### [151] [The Company You Keep: How LLMs Respond to Dark Triad Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04299)
*Zeyi Lu,Angelica Henestrosa,Pavel Chizhov,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究大型语言模型对表达黑暗三联征特质的用户提示的响应模式，发现模型主要表现出纠正行为，但在某些情况下也会强化有害内容，响应行为取决于严重程度和模型类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常表现出高度顺从和强化的对话风格（AI-sycophancy），这种行为在与反映负面社会倾向的用户提示互动时可能变得有问题，可能放大而非减轻有害行为。

Method: 使用精心策划的数据集，研究LLMs对表达不同程度黑暗三联征特质（马基雅维利主义、自恋和精神病态）的用户提示的响应，分析模型行为差异。

Result: 所有模型主要表现出纠正行为，但在某些情况下也显示强化输出；模型行为取决于严重程度，并且在响应情感上存在差异；不同模型之间存在行为差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果对设计更安全的对话系统具有重要意义，需要能够检测并在用户从良性请求升级到有害请求时做出适当响应。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often exhibit highly agreeable and reinforcing conversational styles, also known as AI-sycophancy. Although this behavior is encouraged, it may become problematic when interacting with user prompts that reflect negative social tendencies. Such responses risk amplifying harmful behavior rather than mitigating it. In this study, we examine how LLMs respond to user prompts expressing varying degrees of Dark Triad traits (Machiavellianism, Narcissism, and Psychopathy) using a curated dataset. Our analysis reveals differences across models, whereby all models predominantly exhibit corrective behavior, while showing reinforcing output in certain cases. Model behavior also depends on the severity level and differs in the sentiment of the response. Our findings raise implications for designing safer conversational systems that can detect and respond appropriately when users escalate from benign to harmful requests.

</details>


### [152] [$V_1$: Unifying Generation and Self-Verification for Parallel Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04304)
*Harman Singh,Xiuyu Li,Kusha Sareen,Monishwaran Maheswaran,Sijun Tan,Xiaoxia Wu,Junxiong Wang,Alpay Ariyak,Qingyang Wu,Samir Khaki,Rishabh Tiwari,Long Lian,Yucheng Lu,Boyi Li,Alane Suhr,Ben Athiwaratkun,Kurt Keutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出V₁框架，通过高效的成对排序统一生成与验证，利用模型在成对自我验证方面的优势，显著提升复杂推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算扩展（如采样多个解决方案）能显著提升复杂推理任务性能，但关键瓶颈在于验证：只有能可靠识别正确候选方案时，采样才有效。现有方法通常通过标量评分独立评估候选方案，但研究表明模型在成对自我验证方面表现更强。

Method: 提出V₁框架，包含两个组件：1) V₁-Infer：基于不确定性的锦标赛排名算法，动态分配自我验证计算给相对正确性最不确定的候选对；2) V₁-PairRL：强化学习框架，联合训练单个模型同时作为生成器和成对自我验证器，确保验证器适应生成器不断演化的分布。

Result: 在代码生成（LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、SWE-Bench）和数学推理（AIME、HMMT）基准测试中，V₁-Infer比点式验证提升Pass@1达10%，优于最近的测试时扩展方法且效率更高。V₁-PairRL比标准RL和点式联合训练获得7-9%的测试时扩展增益，在代码生成设置中比标准RL提升基础Pass@1达8.7%。

Conclusion: 模型在成对自我验证方面比独立评分更强，V₁框架通过高效的成对排序统一生成与验证，显著提升了复杂推理任务的性能，为测试时计算扩展提供了更有效的验证机制。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for complex reasoning tasks shows that leveraging inference-time compute, by methods such as independently sampling and aggregating multiple solutions, results in significantly better task outcomes. However, a critical bottleneck is verification: sampling is only effective if correct solutions can be reliably identified among candidates. While existing approaches typically evaluate candidates independently via scalar scoring, we demonstrate that models are substantially stronger at pairwise self-verification. Leveraging this insight, we introduce $V_1$, a framework that unifies generation and verification through efficient pairwise ranking. $V_1$ comprises two components: $V_1$-Infer, an uncertainty-guided algorithm using a tournament-based ranking that dynamically allocates self-verification compute to candidate pairs whose relative correctness is most uncertain; and $V_1$-PairRL, an RL framework that jointly trains a single model as both generator and pairwise self-verifier, ensuring the verifier adapts to the generator's evolving distribution. On code generation (LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, SWE-Bench) and math reasoning (AIME, HMMT) benchmarks, $V_1$-Infer improves Pass@1 by up to $10%$ over pointwise verification and outperforms recent test-time scaling methods while being significantly more efficient. Furthermore, $V_1$-PairRL achieves $7$--$9%$ test-time scaling gains over standard RL and pointwise joint training, and improves base Pass@1 by up to 8.7% over standard RL in a code-generation setting.

</details>


### [153] [World Properties without World Models: Recovering Spatial and Temporal Structure from Co-occurrence Statistics in Static Word Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04317)
*Elan Barenholtz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，从静态词嵌入（GloVe和Word2Vec）中也能线性恢复地理和时间信息，表明这种可恢复性可能源于文本本身的结构而非LLM的内部世界表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为从LLM隐藏状态中线性恢复地理和时间变量表明其具有类世界的内部表征。本文旨在验证一个更简单的可能性：这种结构可能已经潜在地存在于文本本身中。

Method: 使用与先前研究相同的岭回归探针方法，应用于静态共现嵌入（GloVe和Word2Vec）。通过语义邻居分析和目标子空间消融实验，检验信号是否依赖于可解释的词汇梯度。

Result: 从静态嵌入中发现了显著的地理信号（城市坐标的留出R²为0.71-0.87）和较弱但可靠的时间信号（历史出生年份的R²为0.48-0.52）。这些信号主要依赖于可解释的词汇梯度，特别是国家名称和气候相关词汇。

Conclusion: 普通词共现保留了比通常假设更丰富的空间、时间和环境结构，简单的静态嵌入具有从纯文本中保留世界形状结构的显著能力。仅凭线性探针可恢复性不能证明表征超越了文本本身。

Abstract: Recent work interprets the linear recoverability of geographic and temporal variables from large language model (LLM) hidden states as evidence for world-like internal representations. We test a simpler possibility: that much of the relevant structure is already latent in text itself. Applying the same class of ridge regression probes to static co-occurrence-based embeddings (GloVe and Word2Vec), we find substantial recoverable geographic signal and weaker but reliable temporal signal, with held-out R^2 values of 0.71-0.87 for city coordinates and 0.48-0.52 for historical birth years. Semantic-neighbor analyses and targeted subspace ablations show that these signals depend strongly on interpretable lexical gradients, especially country names and climate-related vocabulary. These findings suggest that ordinary word co-occurrence preserves richer spatial, temporal, and environmental structure than is often assumed, revealing a remarkable and underappreciated capacity of simple static embeddings to preserve world-shaped structure from text alone. Linear probe recoverability alone therefore does not establish a representational move beyond text.

</details>


### [154] [AILS-NTUA at SemEval-2026 Task 12: Graph-Based Retrieval and Reflective Prompting for Abductive Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04319)
*Nikolas Karafyllis,Maria Lymperaiou,Giorgos Filandrianos,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于溯因事件推理的三阶段系统，在SemEval 2026 Task 12中排名第一，准确率达0.95；同时通过跨模型错误分析揭示了多标签因果推理中的系统性偏差模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多标签因果推理任务中的挑战，开发一个能够在SemEval 2026 Task 12中取得优异表现的溯因事件推理系统，同时深入理解不同模型在该任务中的系统性失败模式。

Method: 采用三阶段系统：1) 基于图的检索；2) 基于LLM的溯因推理，通过反思式提示演化优化提示设计；3) 事后一致性强化。同时进行了跨模型错误分析，涵盖14个模型（7个家族）。

Result: 系统在评估阶段排行榜上排名第一，准确率得分为0.95。跨模型分析揭示了三个共享的归纳偏差：因果链不完整性、近因偏好和显著性偏差，这些偏差在跨家族中表现出收敛性（原因计数减少51%），表明是多标签因果推理中的系统性而非模型特定的失败模式。

Conclusion: 提出的三阶段系统在溯因事件推理任务中表现出色，同时揭示的多标签因果推理中的系统性偏差为未来改进提供了重要见解，这些偏差的跨模型一致性表明需要更根本的解决方案来应对因果推理的挑战。

Abstract: We present a winning three-stage system for SemEval 2026 Task~12: Abductive Event Reasoning that combines graph-based retrieval, LLM-driven abductive reasoning with prompt design optimized through reflective prompt evolution, and post-hoc consistency enforcement; our system ranks first on the evaluation-phase leaderboard with an accuracy score of 0.95. Cross-model error analysis across 14 models (7~families) reveals three shared inductive biases: causal chain incompleteness, proximate cause preference, and salience bias, whose cross-family convergence (51\% cause-count reduction) indicates systematic rather than model-specific failure modes in multi-label causal reasoning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [The Theory behind UMAP?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03375)
*David Wegmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文旨在修正Spivak未发表草稿中的错误，这些错误被McInnes等人引入的UMAP算法所继承，并提供完整的度量实现函子推导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP算法基于Spivak未发表的草稿，该草稿包含许多错误，这些错误被McInnes等人和后续出版物所复制。论文旨在修复这些错误，为相关数学基础提供完整、自包含的推导

Method: 提供度量实现函子及相关函子的显式描述，完整推导Spivak的函子和McInnes等人的有限变体，并对UMAP算法及其性质进行讨论

Result: 修正了原始草稿中的错误，提供了完整的数学推导，澄清了度量实现函子的定义，并讨论了UMAP算法与其理论基础之间的对应关系

Conclusion: 论文成功修复了Spivak草稿中的错误，为UMAP算法的数学基础提供了严谨的推导，有助于更准确地理解该算法的理论基础和实现细节

Abstract: In 2018, McInnes et al. introduced a dimensionality reduction algorithm called UMAP, which enjoys wide popularity among data scientists. Their work introduces a finite variant of a functor called the metric realization, based on an unpublished draft by Spivak. This draft contains many errors, most of which are reproduced by McInnes et al. and subsequent publications. This article aims to repair these errors and provide a self-contained document with the full derivation of Spivak's functors and McInnes et al.'s finite variant. We contribute an explicit description of the metric realization and related functors. At the end, we discuss the UMAP algorithm, as well as claims about properties of the algorithm and the correspondence of McInnes et al.'s finite variant to the UMAP algorithm.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Order Forest for Qualitative-Attribute Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03387)
*Mingjie Zhao,Sen Feng,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于树状距离结构的聚类方法，用于处理定性属性值（如症状、婚姻状况等）的聚类问题，通过联合学习机制迭代优化树结构和聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法通常基于欧氏距离空间，但这不适用于反映定性属性值的隐式聚类分布。定性属性值（如名义值）缺乏直观的距离度量，需要一种灵活表示属性内定性值间局部顺序关系的方法。

Method: 提出树状距离结构来表示属性内定性值间的局部顺序关系，将每个值视为树的顶点。采用联合学习机制迭代优化树结构和聚类结果，最终整个数据集的潜在距离空间由学习得到的树构成的森林表示。

Result: 在12个真实基准数据集上与10种对比方法进行比较，并通过显著性检验验证了所提方法的优越性。实验表明联合学习机制能使森林适应聚类任务，产生准确的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究成功发现了一种树状距离结构来灵活表示定性属性值间的局部顺序关系，通过联合学习机制获得的森林能很好地表示整个数据集的潜在距离空间，在聚类任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental approach to understanding data patterns, wherein the intuitive Euclidean distance space is commonly adopted. However, this is not the case for implicit cluster distributions reflected by qualitative attribute values, e.g., the nominal values of attributes like symptoms, marital status, etc. This paper, therefore, discovered a tree-like distance structure to flexibly represent the local order relationship among intra-attribute qualitative values. That is, treating a value as the vertex of the tree allows to capture rich order relationships among the vertex value and the others. To obtain the trees in a clustering-friendly form, a joint learning mechanism is proposed to iteratively obtain more appropriate tree structures and clusters. It turns out that the latent distance space of the whole dataset can be well-represented by a forest consisting of the learned trees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the joint learning adapts the forest to the clustering task to yield accurate results. Comparisons of 10 counterparts on 12 real benchmark datasets with significance tests verify the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Cross-Validation: Adaptive Parameter Selection for Kernel-Based Gradient Descents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03401)
*Xiaotong Liu,Yunwen Lei,Xiangyu Chang,Shao-Bo Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于偏差-方差分析和分裂方法的核梯度下降参数选择策略，引入经验有效维度概念，实现自适应参数选择，在理论上证明能达到最优泛化误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的核梯度下降（KGD）算法参数选择方法存在不足，需要一种能够自适应不同核函数、目标函数和误差度量，并能达到最优泛化性能的参数选择策略。

Method: 结合偏差-方差分析和分裂方法，引入经验有效维度概念来量化KGD迭代增量，提出可实施的自适应参数选择策略，并使用积分算子方法进行理论验证。

Result: 理论证明KGD算法配备所提出的自适应参数选择策略能够达到最优泛化误差界，并能有效适应不同的核函数、目标函数和误差度量，优于现有参数选择方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应参数选择策略在理论上具有优越性，能够为核梯度下降算法提供更优的参数选择方案，提升算法性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel parameter selection strategy for kernel-based gradient descent (KGD) algorithms, integrating bias-variance analysis with the splitting method. We introduce the concept of empirical effective dimension to quantify iteration increments in KGD, deriving an adaptive parameter selection strategy that is implementable. Theoretical verifications are provided within the framework of learning theory. Utilizing the recently developed integral operator approach, we rigorously demonstrate that KGD, equipped with the proposed adaptive parameter selection strategy, achieves the optimal generalization error bound and adapts effectively to different kernels, target functions, and error metrics. Consequently, this strategy showcases significant advantages over existing parameter selection methods for KGD.

</details>


### [158] [Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03405)
*Shion Matsumoto,Raul Castillo,Benjamin Prada,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Surprisal-Rényi自由能(SRFE)，这是一个基于对数矩的似然比泛函，能够统一解释前向和反向KL散度的极限行为，揭示了均值-方差权衡机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向和反向KL散度在学习和推理中作为极限目标出现，但产生明显不同的归纳偏差，这些偏差无法仅通过期望水平来解释。需要一个新的理论框架来理解这两种KL散度极限的几何和大偏差结构。

Method: 引入Surprisal-Rényi自由能(SRFE)，这是一个位于f-散度类之外的基于对数矩的似然比泛函。通过局部展开分析SRFE在KL极限附近的行为，推导变分表征，并建立与Chernoff型边界和最小描述长度原理的联系。

Result: SRFE能够恢复前向和反向KL散度作为奇异端点极限，并在两个极限附近展开中显示对数似然比方差作为一阶修正。建立了SRFE的Gibbs型变分表征，证明其控制超额编码长度的大偏差，提供了精确的最小描述长度解释。

Conclusion: SRFE作为一个对方差和尾部敏感的自由能泛函，阐明了前向和反向KL极限的几何和大偏差结构，揭示了控制偏离KL主导机制的显式均值-方差权衡，而不试图统一或取代不同的学习框架。

Abstract: The forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergences arise as limiting objectives in learning and inference yet induce markedly different inductive biases that cannot be explained at the level of expectations alone. In this work, we introduce the Surprisal-Rényi Free Energy (SRFE), a log-moment-based functional of the likelihood ratio that lies outside the class of $f$-divergences. We show that SRFE recovers forward and reverse KL divergences as singular endpoint limits and derive local expansions around both limits in which the variance of the log-likelihood ratio appears as a first-order correction. This reveals an explicit mean-variance tradeoff governing departures from KL-dominated regimes. We further establish a Gibbs-type variational characterization of SRFE as the unique minimizer of a weighted sum of KL divergences and prove that SRFE directly controls large deviations of excess code-length via Chernoff-type bounds, yielding a precise Minimum Description Length interpretation. Together, these results identify SRFE as a variance- and tail-sensitive free-energy functional that clarifies the geometric and large-deviation structure underlying forward and reverse KL limits, without unifying or subsuming distinct learning frameworks.

</details>


### [159] [Scalable Contrastive Causal Discovery under Unknown Soft Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03411)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Khushi Desai,Sopho Kevlishvili,Elham Azizi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的因果发现模型，用于处理配对观测和干预设置，其中干预是软干预且目标未知，通过聚合子集级PDAG和应用对比性跨机制定向规则来构建全局一致的极大PDAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测因果发现只能识别到马尔可夫等价类，而干预可以减少这种模糊性。然而实践中干预通常是软干预且目标未知，且通常只能观察到单一干预机制。需要一种能处理这种现实场景的因果发现方法。

Method: 提出一个可扩展模型，用于配对观测和干预设置，具有共享的底层因果结构和未知软干预。模型聚合子集级PDAG，应用对比性跨机制定向规则，在Meek闭包下构建全局一致的极大PDAG，支持分布内和分布外泛化。

Result: 理论证明模型在子集限制设置诱导的受限Ψ等价类中是完备的，渐近恢复可识别的PDAG，相比非对比性子集限制方法能定向更多边。合成数据实验显示改进的因果结构恢复、对未见图的泛化能力以及扩展到更大图的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该模型为处理现实世界中软干预、未知干预目标和单一干预机制的因果发现问题提供了有效的解决方案，通过对比性跨机制定向实现了更好的结构恢复和泛化能力。

Abstract: Observational causal discovery is only identifiable up to the Markov equivalence class. While interventions can reduce this ambiguity, in practice interventions are often soft with multiple unknown targets. In many realistic scenarios, only a single intervention regime is observed. We propose a scalable causal discovery model for paired observational and interventional settings with shared underlying causal structure and unknown soft interventions. The model aggregates subset-level PDAGs and applies contrastive cross-regime orientation rules to construct a globally consistent maximal PDAG under Meek closure, enabling generalization to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Theoretically, we prove that our model is sound with respect to a restricted $Ψ$ equivalence class induced solely by the information available in the subset-restricted setting. We further show that the model asymptotically recovers the corresponding identifiable PDAG and can orient additional edges compared to non-contrastive subset-restricted methods. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate improved causal structure recovery, generalization to unseen graphs with held-out causal mechanisms, and scalability to larger graphs, with ablations supporting the theoretical results.

</details>


### [160] [Empirical Evaluation of No Free Lunch Violations in Permutation-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03613)
*Grzegorz Sroka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文探讨了No Free Lunch定理的局限性，指出在均匀采样闭置换函数空间下平均性能相等的保证，但在实际基准测试中可能失效。通过迭代搜索设置研究代数重构如何影响算法性能排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究No Free Lunch定理在实际基准测试中的适用性限制，探讨函数空间采样方式和目标函数代数重构如何影响算法性能评估，揭示基准设计对算法选择的重要性。

Method: 采用迭代搜索设置，研究无放回采样下仅评估顺序不同的算法。使用二进制目标函数实现完全枚举，通过和与差的代数运算重构基准函数。通过相关性结构、层次聚类、delta热图、PCA分析函数-算法关系，使用单因素方差分析和Tukey对比验证统计显著性。

Result: 均匀采样基准保持与全局NFL对称性一致，而代数修改的基准产生稳定的重新排名和一致的函数-采样策略聚类。复合目标函数表现出非加性搜索努力。蒙特卡洛实验表明排序效应在更大空间中持续存在且依赖于函数类别。

Conclusion: 目标函数重构和基准设计能够产生结构化局部偏离NFL直觉的结果，强调算法选择需要考虑问题类别和目标表示形式。这一结论适用于进化计算以及基于重标记、重采样和置换检验的统计程序。

Abstract: The No Free Lunch (NFL) theorem guarantees equal average performance only under uniform sampling of a function space closed under permutation (c.u.p.). We ask when this averaging ceases to reflect what benchmarking actually reports. We study an iterative-search setting with sampling without replacement, where algorithms differ only in evaluation order. Binary objectives allow exhaustive evaluation in the fully enumerable case, and efficiency is defined by the first time the global minimum is reached. We then construct two additional benchmarks by algebraically recombining the same baseline functions through sums and differences. Function-algorithm relations are examined via correlation structure, hierarchical clustering, delta heatmaps, and PCA. A one-way ANOVA with Tukey contrasts confirms that algebraic reformulations induce statistically meaningful shifts in performance patterns. The uniformly sampled baseline remains consistent with the global NFL symmetry. In contrast, the algebraically modified benchmarks yield stable re-rankings and coherent clusters of functions and sampling policies. Composite objectives can also exhibit non-additive search effort despite being built from simpler components. Monte Carlo experiments indicate that order effects persist in larger spaces and depend on function class. Taken together, the results show how objective reformulation and benchmark design can generate structured local departures from NFL intuition. They motivate algorithm choice that is aware of both the problem class and the objective representation. This message applies to evolutionary computation as well as to statistical procedures based on relabeling, resampling, and permutation tests.

</details>


### [161] [Riemannian Langevin Dynamics: Strong Convergence of Geometric Euler-Maruyama Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03626)
*Zhiyuan Zhan,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究了黎曼流形上随机微分方程的几何欧拉-丸山格式，证明了其在几何和正则性条件下的1/2阶强收敛性，并应用于流形采样


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据中的低维结构对生成模型成功至关重要，这推动了在内在数据流形上定义扩散模型的需求。这类模型由流形上的随机微分方程驱动，因此需要流形值SDE数值格式的收敛理论。在欧几里得空间中，欧拉-丸山格式能达到1/2阶强收敛，但在一般设置下，流形离散化的类似结果较少被理解。

Method: 研究黎曼流形上SDE的几何欧拉-丸山格式，在几何和正则性条件下分析其收敛性。该方法将欧几里得空间的欧拉-丸山格式推广到黎曼流形上，考虑流形的几何结构对离散化方案的影响。

Result: 证明了在适当的几何和正则性条件下，黎曼流形上SDE的几何欧拉-丸山格式能达到1/2阶强收敛。作为应用，通过黎曼朗之万动力学的几何EM离散化，获得了流形上采样的Wasserstein界。

Conclusion: 该工作为流形上随机微分方程的数值离散化提供了收敛理论保证，特别是证明了几何欧拉-丸山格式的1/2阶强收敛性。这一理论结果支持了在内在数据流形上构建扩散模型的数值实现，并为流形采样算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Low-dimensional structure in real-world data plays an important role in the success of generative models, which motivates diffusion models defined on intrinsic data manifolds. Such models are driven by stochastic differential equations (SDEs) on manifolds, which raises the need for convergence theory of numerical schemes for manifold-valued SDEs. In Euclidean space, the Euler--Maruyama (EM) scheme achieves strong convergence with order $1/2$, but an analogous result for manifold discretizations is less understood in general settings. In this work, we study a geometric version of the EM scheme for SDEs on Riemannian manifolds and prove strong convergence with order $1/2$ under geometric and regularity conditions. As an application, we obtain a Wasserstein bound for sampling on manifolds via the geometric EM discretization of Riemannian Langevin dynamics.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Mixtures and Products for Ensemble Aggregation: A Likelihood Perspective on Generalized Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04204)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Damien Garreau,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了密度聚合问题，通过归一化广义均值框架分析不同聚合方法（线性池化与几何池化）在机器学习中的性能表现，发现r∈[0,1]是唯一能确保系统性改进的参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中密度聚合是一个核心问题，特别是在深度集成中组合预测时。目前存在线性池化（概率平均）和几何池化（logit平均）两种常用方法，但哪种方法更优仍是一个开放性问题。作者希望通过研究归一化广义均值在log-likelihood评估标准下的表现，为聚合方法选择提供理论依据。

Method: 使用归一化广义均值（order r ∈ ℝ ∪ {-∞,+∞}）作为统一的聚合形式化框架，通过log-likelihood这一标准机器学习评估准则进行分析。理论分析不同r值对应的聚合性能，并通过深度集成在图像和文本分类基准上进行实证验证。

Result: 研究表明，r∈[0,1]是唯一能确保相对于个体分布系统性改进的参数范围，这为线性池化（r=1）和几何池化（r=0）的可靠性和广泛应用提供了原则性依据。相反，r∉[0,1]的聚合规则可能无法提供一致的性能提升，并给出了明确的反例。实证结果与理论发现一致。

Conclusion: 归一化广义均值为密度聚合提供了统一的形式化框架，并确定了r∈[0,1]作为确保系统性改进的最优参数范围。这为深度集成中线性池化和几何池化的选择提供了理论指导，表明这两种常用方法在理论上是合理的。

Abstract: Density aggregation is a central problem in machine learning, for instance when combining predictions from a Deep Ensemble. The choice of aggregation remains an open question with two commonly proposed approaches being linear pooling (probability averaging) and geometric pooling (logit averaging). In this work, we address this question by studying the normalized generalized mean of order $r \in \mathbb{R} \cup \{-\infty,+\infty\}$ through the lens of log-likelihood, the standard evaluation criterion in machine learning. This provides a unifying aggregation formalism and shows different optimal configurations for different situations. We show that the regime $r \in [0,1]$ is the only range ensuring systematic improvements relative to individual distributions, thereby providing a principled justification for the reliability and widespread practical use of linear ($r=1$) and geometric ($r=0$) pooling. In contrast, we show that aggregation rules with $r \notin [0,1]$ may fail to provide consistent gains with explicit counterexamples. Finally, we corroborate our theoretical findings with empirical evaluations using Deep Ensembles on image and text classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Observationally Informed Adaptive Causal Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03785)
*Erdun Gao,Liang Zhang,Jake Fawkes,Aoqi Zuo,Wenqin Liu,Haoxuan Li,Mingming Gong,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Active Residual Learning新范式，利用观测模型作为先验，将实验重点从从头学习因果量转向高效估计修正观测偏差所需的残差，并通过R-Design框架实现，在理论和实验上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验(RCTs)是因果推断的金标准但稀缺，大规模观测数据仅用于回顾性融合，因偏差问题在前瞻性试验设计中常被丢弃。这种"白板"数据获取策略效率低下，需要新方法利用观测数据提高试验设计效率。

Method: 提出Active Residual Learning范式，利用观测模型作为基础先验，将实验重点转向高效估计修正观测偏差所需的残差。引入R-Design框架，包括R-EPIG（残差期望预测信息增益）准则，直接针对因果估计量，最小化残差不确定性以进行估计或澄清决策边界。

Result: 理论证明两个关键优势：(1)结构效率差距：估计平滑残差对比比重建完整结果收敛速度更快；(2)信息效率：量化标准参数获取中的冗余，证明基线方法在任务无关的冗余不确定性上浪费预算。在合成和半合成基准测试中，R-Design显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 修复有偏差模型比从头学习模型更高效。Active Residual Learning通过利用观测模型作为先验，将实验资源集中于估计修正偏差所需的残差，显著提高了试验设计效率，为因果推断的试验设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Randomized Controlled Trials (RCTs) represent the gold standard for causal inference yet remain a scarce resource. While large-scale observational data is often available, it is utilized only for retrospective fusion, and remains discarded in prospective trial design due to bias concerns. We argue this "tabula rasa" data acquisition strategy is fundamentally inefficient. In this work, we propose Active Residual Learning, a new paradigm that leverages the observational model as a foundational prior. This approach shifts the experimental focus from learning target causal quantities from scratch to efficiently estimating the residuals required to correct observational bias. To operationalize this, we introduce the R-Design framework. Theoretically, we establish two key advantages: (1) a structural efficiency gap, proving that estimating smooth residual contrasts admits strictly faster convergence rates than reconstructing full outcomes; and (2) information efficiency, where we quantify the redundancy in standard parameter-based acquisition (e.g., BALD), demonstrating that such baselines waste budget on task-irrelevant nuisance uncertainty. We propose R-EPIG (Residual Expected Predictive Information Gain), a unified criterion that directly targets the causal estimand, minimizing residual uncertainty for estimation or clarifying decision boundaries for policy. Experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that R-Design significantly outperforms baselines, confirming that repairing a biased model is far more efficient than learning one from scratch.

</details>


### [164] [Invariance-Based Dynamic Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03843)
*Margherita Lazzaretto,Jonas Peters,Niklas Pfister*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ISD-linUCB算法，通过分解奖励模型为平稳和非平稳分量，利用历史数据学习不变性特征，在快速变化环境中显著降低遗憾


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平稳线性赌博机算法通过丢弃或降权历史数据来定位策略，但历史数据可能仍包含部分奖励模型信息。需要一种既能适应变化又能利用历史数据的方法。

Method: 假设奖励模型可分解为平稳和非平稳分量，提出ISD-linUCB算法，使用历史数据学习奖励模型中的不变性特征，并利用这些不变性改进在线性能

Result: 理论和实证表明，利用不变性特征可降低问题维度，在快速变化环境中，当有足够历史数据时能显著减少遗憾

Conclusion: 通过分解奖励模型并利用历史数据学习不变性特征，可以在非平稳线性赌博机问题中实现更好的性能，特别是在快速变化环境中

Abstract: We consider stochastic non-stationary linear bandits where the linear parameter connecting contexts to the reward changes over time. Existing algorithms in this setting localize the policy by gradually discarding or down-weighting past data, effectively shrinking the time horizon over which learning can occur. However, in many settings historical data may still carry partial information about the reward model. We propose to leverage such data while adapting to changes, by assuming the reward model decomposes into stationary and non-stationary components. Based on this assumption, we introduce ISD-linUCB, an algorithm that uses past data to learn invariances in the reward model and subsequently exploits them to improve online performance. We show both theoretically and empirically that leveraging invariance reduces the problem dimensionality, yielding significant regret improvements in fast-changing environments when sufficient historical data is available.

</details>


### [165] [Stable and Steerable Sparse Autoencoders with Weight Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04198)
*Piotr Jedryszek,Oliver M. Crook*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: L2权重正则化显著提升稀疏自编码器特征稳定性，增强跨随机种子特征一致性，改善特征解释与功能可控性对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器提取的特征在不同随机种子和训练选择下变化很大，需要提高特征稳定性

Method: 研究L1和L2权重正则化对编码器和解码器权重的惩罚，评估正则化与常见SAE训练默认设置的交互作用

Result: L2正则化产生高度对齐的核心特征，结合绑定初始化和单位范数解码器约束显著提升跨种子特征一致性；在语言模型上，小L2惩罚增加跨种子共享特征比例，使转向成功率翻倍，同时保持自动可解释性分数均值不变

Conclusion: 正则化能提高稀疏自编码器特征稳定性，使基于文本的特征解释与功能可控性更好对齐

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract human-interpretable features from neural network activations, but their learned features can vary substantially across random seeds and training choices. To improve stability, we studied weight regularization by adding L1 or L2 penalties on encoder and decoder weights, and evaluate how regularization interacts with common SAE training defaults. On MNIST, we observe that L2 weight regularization produces a core of highly aligned features and, when combined with tied initialization and unit-norm decoder constraints, it dramatically increases cross-seed feature consistency. For TopK SAEs trained on language model activations (Pythia-70M-deduped), adding a small L2 weight penalty increased the fraction of features shared across three random seeds and roughly doubles steering success rates, while leaving the mean of automated interpretability scores essentially unchanged. Finally, in the regularized setting, activation steering success becomes better predicted by auto-interpretability scores, suggesting that regularization can align text-based feature explanations with functional controllability.

</details>


### [166] [Semi-Supervised Generative Learning via Latent Space Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04223)
*Kwong Yu Chong,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LSDM是一种新颖的半监督条件分布生成建模框架，通过两阶段方法在潜在空间中进行联合分布匹配，减少对配对数据的依赖，实现快速一步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决条件生成任务中配对数据稀缺的问题，同时利用未配对数据提升生成质量，为潜在空间方法提供统一的统计视角。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）从配对和未配对数据学习低维潜在空间；2）在潜在空间中使用1-Wasserstein距离进行联合分布匹配。该方法最小化了联合分布之间1-Wasserstein距离的上界。

Result: 建立了非渐近误差界，证明了未配对数据能增强生成输出的几何保真度。在真实图像任务（类条件生成和图像超分辨率）上验证了LSDM利用未配对数据提升生成质量的有效性。

Conclusion: LSDM为潜在空间方法提供了统一的统计框架，将潜在扩散模型视为其变体，并为LDMs的一致性提供了理论见解。该框架在减少配对数据依赖的同时实现了高质量的条件生成。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Space Distribution Matching (LSDM), a novel framework for semi-supervised generative modeling of conditional distributions. LSDM operates in two stages: (i) learning a low-dimensional latent space from both paired and unpaired data, and (ii) performing joint distribution matching in this space via the 1-Wasserstein distance, using only paired data. This two-step approach minimizes an upper bound on the 1-Wasserstein distance between joint distributions, reducing reliance on scarce paired samples while enabling fast one-step generation. Theoretically, we establish non-asymptotic error bounds and demonstrate a key benefit of unpaired data: enhanced geometric fidelity in generated outputs. Furthermore, by extending the scope of its two core steps, LSDM provides a coherent statistical perspective that connects to a broad class of latent-space approaches. Notably, Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) can be viewed as a variant of LSDM, in which joint distribution matching is achieved indirectly via score matching. Consequently, our results also provide theoretical insights into the consistency of LDMs. Empirical evaluations on real-world image tasks, including class-conditional generation and image super-resolution, demonstrate the effectiveness of LSDM in leveraging unpaired data to enhance generation quality.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [On large bandwidth matrix values kernel smoothed estimators for multi-index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03763)
*Taku Moriyama*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 核平滑方法在大带宽下通常会导致过平滑或欠拟合，但当包含无关变量时，大带宽具有收缩无关变量的效果。本文研究了多指数模型下核条件密度估计器和回归估计器在大带宽矩阵元素情况下的渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究核平滑方法中带宽选择对估计性能的影响，特别是在包含无关变量的情况下。传统上大带宽会导致过平滑，但作者发现大带宽对无关变量具有收缩效应，这可能有助于缓解维度诅咒问题。

Method: 采用核条件密度估计器和回归估计器，研究其在大带宽矩阵元素下的渐近性质。针对多指数模型，分析估计器的最优收敛速率与变量数量及有效维度的关系。通过数值研究验证有限样本性能，并讨论带宽选择方法。

Result: 发现估计器的最优收敛速率不依赖于变量总数，而仅依赖于有效维度，即使不消除无关变量也能实现。这表明核条件密度估计器和回归估计器本质上具备缓解维度诅咒的能力。数值研究和波士顿住房数据的案例研究验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 核平滑方法在大带宽下对无关变量具有收缩效应，使得估计器的最优收敛速率仅依赖于有效维度而非总变量数。这为高维数据分析提供了一种自然的维度约简方法，无需显式地消除无关变量。

Abstract: The kernel smoothing with large bandwidth values causes oversmoothing or underfitting in general. However, when irrelevant variables are included, the corresponding large bandwidth values are known to have an effect of shrinking them. This study investigates asymptotic properties of the kernel conditional density estimator and the regression estimator with large bandwidth matrix elements for cases of multi-index model. It is clarified that the optimal convergence rate of the estimators depends on not the number of the variables but the effective dimension without eliminating the irrelevant variables. Thus, the kernel conditional density estimator and regression estimator are demonstrated to equip the reduction of the curse of dimensionality by nature. Finite sample performances are investigated by a numerical study, and the bandwidth selection is discussed. Finally a case study on the Boston housing data is provided.

</details>


### [168] [On the generalized circular projected Cauchy distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04030)
*Omar Alzeley,Michail Tsagris*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出广义圆形投影柯西分布，建立其与包裹柯西分布的关系，并开发用于检验两个角均值相等的对数似然比检验方法，无需假设浓度参数相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有包裹柯西分布是广义圆形投影柯西分布的特例，需要建立两者关系并开发更灵活的统计检验方法，避免错误假设分布类型导致的检验偏差。

Method: 首先推导广义圆形投影柯西分布与包裹柯西分布的关系，然后提出用于检验两个角均值相等的对数似然比检验，该检验不要求浓度参数相等，并通过模拟研究验证方法性能。

Result: 建立了两种分布的理论关系，开发了灵活的角均值检验方法，模拟研究表明当错误假设真实分布为包裹柯西分布时，所提检验方法仍能保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 广义圆形投影柯西分布为圆形数据分析提供了更灵活的框架，所提对数似然比检验方法在分布假设不准确时仍具有鲁棒性，扩展了圆形统计推断工具。

Abstract: \cite{tsagris2025a} proposed the generalized circular projected Cauchy distribution, whose special case is the wrapped Cauchy distribution. In this paper we first derive the relationship with the wrapped Cauchy distribution and we propose a log-likelihood ratio test for the equality of two angular means, without assuming equality of thew concentration parameters. Simulation studies illustrate the performance of the test when one falsely assumes that the true underlying distribution is the wrapped Cauchy distribution.

</details>


### [169] [The Pivotal Information Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04172)
*Sylvain Sardy,Maxime van Cutsem,Sara van de Geer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: PIC（枢轴信息准则）通过连续优化和基于检测边界的惩罚参数选择，解决了传统信息准则（BIC/AIC）惩罚过小导致假发现率高、离散优化在高维不可行的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）和赤池信息准则（AIC）存在两个主要问题：1）惩罚参数（λ=log n和λ=2）过小，导致大量假发现；2）固有的离散优化（最佳子集选择）在高维情况下不可行。需要一种能更好平衡欠拟合和过拟合的准则。

Method: 提出枢轴信息准则（PIC）：1）定义为连续优化问题而非离散优化；2）惩罚参数λ在纯噪声条件下的检测边界处选择；3）证明在适当变换损失函数后，相关统计量是（渐近）枢轴的；4）λ选择为该枢轴统计量的分位数。

Result: 仿真显示PIC在精确支持恢复概率上存在相变现象（类似压缩感知中无噪声情况的研究）。在真实数据应用中，在相似预测性能下，PIC选择了最简模型（相比现有先进方法）。

Conclusion: PIC通过连续优化框架和基于检测边界的惩罚参数选择，有效解决了传统信息准则惩罚不足和计算不可行的问题，在保持预测性能的同时实现了更简约的模型选择。

Abstract: The Bayesian and Akaike information criteria aim at finding a good balance between under- and over-fitting. They are extensively used every day by practitioners. Yet we contend they suffer from at least two afflictions: their penalty parameter $λ=\log n$ and $λ=2$ are too small, leading to many false discoveries, and their inherent (best subset) discrete optimization is infeasible in high dimension. We alleviate these issues with the pivotal information criterion: PIC is defined as a continuous optimization problem, and the PIC penalty parameter $λ$ is selected at the detection boundary (under pure noise). PIC's choice of $λ$ is the quantile of a statistic that we prove to be (asymptotically) pivotal, provided the loss function is appropriately transformed. As a result, simulations show a phase transition in the probability of exact support recovery with PIC, a phenomenon studied with no noise in compressed sensing. Applied on real data, for similar predictive performances, PIC selects the least complex model among state-of-the-art learners.

</details>


### [170] [Bayesian Adversarial Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04199)
*Cameron Bell,Timothy Johnston,Antoine Luciano,Christian P Robert*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种新的定量隐私定义，基于贝叶斯决策理论但有所创新，强调从先验视角而非数据条件视角进行披露决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私研究框架多样，需要更具体、更严谨的隐私定义。差分隐私框架虽然广泛应用，但不够有意义；统计披露理论常用方法不够明确和严格

Method: 提出基于标准贝叶斯决策理论的新隐私定义，但有几个重要创新：控制敏感信息发布的方应从先验视角而非数据条件视角做出披露决策，即使数据本身已被观测

Result: 通过详细的启发式示例和计算方法展示了该方法的特性，证明了新定义比差分隐私更有意义，比统计披露理论更明确严谨

Conclusion: 该工作引入了一种新的定量隐私概念，既是情境化的又是具体的，为隐私保护提供了更优的理论框架

Abstract: Theoretical and applied research into privacy encompasses an incredibly broad swathe of differing approaches, emphasis and aims. This work introduces a new quantitative notion of privacy that is both contextual and specific. We argue that it provides a more meaningful notion of privacy than the widely utilised framework of differential privacy and a more explicit and rigorous formulation than what is commonly used in statistical disclosure theory. Our definition relies on concepts inherent to standard Bayesian decision theory, while departing from it in several important respects. In particular, the party controlling the release of sensitive information should make disclosure decisions from the prior viewpoint, rather than conditional on the data, even when the data is itself observed. Illuminating toy examples and computational methods are discussed in high detail in order to highlight the specificities of the method.

</details>


### [171] [Extreme Geometric Quantiles Under Minimal Assumptions, with a Connection to Tukey Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04347)
*Sibsankar Singha,Marie Kratz,Sreekar Vadlamani*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了几何（空间）分位数的极值行为，建立了无需矩条件的极端几何分位数范数的一般上下界，并探讨了这些结果对分布行为表征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何分位数作为多元分位数的三种主要定义方法之一，其极值行为尚未得到充分研究。作者旨在建立极端几何分位数范数的一般界限，无需任何矩条件，并探索这些结果对分布行为表征的意义。

Method: 建立极端几何分位数范数的一般下界和上界，这些界限不依赖于任何矩条件。特别关注下界与一元分位数以及半空间（Tukey）深度中心区域的直接联系。

Result: 获得了极端几何分位数范数的一般下界和上界，这些界限无需矩条件。发现下界可以与一元分位数和半空间（Tukey）深度中心区域直接关联，揭示了这两种基本多元分位数概念之间的新颖联系。

Conclusion: 该研究为几何分位数的极值行为提供了新的理论框架，建立了无需矩条件的一般界限，并揭示了几何分位数与半空间深度之间的重要联系，这对多元分位数理论和分布行为表征具有重要意义。

Abstract: Geometric (also known as spatial) quantiles, introduced by Chaudhury and representing one of the three principal approaches to defining multivariate quantiles, have been well studied in the literature. In this work, we focus on the extremal behaviour of these quantiles. We establish new extremal properties, namely general lower and upper bounds for the norm of extreme geometric quantiles, free of any moment conditions. We discuss the impact of such results on the characterization of distribution behaviour. Importantly, the lower bound can be directly linked to univariate quantiles and to halfspace (Tukey) depth central regions, highlighting a novel connection between these two fundamental notions of multivariate quantiles.

</details>


### [172] [On the singularity of the Fisher Information matrix in the sine-skewed family on the d-dimensional torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04369)
*Emily Schutte,Sophia Loizidou,Vincent Laheurte*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维环面上正弦偏斜分布的Fisher信息矩阵奇异性问题，给出了在对称性附近出现奇异性的一般特征化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正弦偏斜机制是环面上唯一提出的偏斜机制，但已知某些正弦偏斜模型在对称性附近存在Fisher信息矩阵奇异性，这对推断目的构成重大问题。确定哪些正弦偏斜模型会出现Fisher信息奇异性是一个开放性问题。

Method: 在一般d维设置下，给出了展示这种奇异性的模型类别的一般特征化。通过理论分析确定了正弦偏斜模型在对称性附近Fisher信息矩阵出现奇异性的条件。

Result: 提供了正弦偏斜模型在对称性附近Fisher信息矩阵奇异性的一般特征化，解决了确定哪些模型会出现这一问题的开放性问题。

Conclusion: 本文成功表征了d维环面上正弦偏斜模型中Fisher信息矩阵在对称性附近出现奇异性的模型类别，为相关统计推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Skewed distributions are fundamental in modelling asymmetric data on the d-dimensional torus. In this context, asymmetry is introduced through the sine-skewing mechanism, which is the only skewing mechanism that has been proposed on the hyper-torus in the literature. Some sine-skewed models are known to suffer from a singular Fisher information matrix in the vicinity of symmetry, which poses a significant issue for inferential purposes. It is an open question to determine for which sine-skewed models Fisher information singularity occurs. In this paper, a general characterization of the class of models that exhibit this singularity is given in the general d-dimensional setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Designing with Medical Mistrust: Perspectives from Black Older Adults in Publicly Subsidized Housing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03416)
*Cynthia M. Baseman,Reeda Shimaz Huda,Rosa I. Arriaga*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨医疗不信任作为健康社会决定因素，聚焦低收入黑人老年群体，通过访谈分析医疗不信任的社区视角，提出基于黑人女性主义思想的设计原则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对文化敏感性健康技术的兴趣日益增长，但医疗不信任在人本计算领域仍未得到充分探索。医疗不信任被视为健康的社会决定因素，是基于历史背景、结构性不平等和歧视的理性保护性反应。研究旨在以种族为基础的医疗不信任和低收入黑人老年人的生活经验为中心

Method: 在美国南部公共补贴住房中对黑人老年人进行访谈，采用反思性主题分析方法描述社区对医疗保健和医疗不信任的视角，包括认证与体现、对财务动机的怀疑以及对健康AI意图的质疑。提供反思性练习让研究人员考虑其在社区参与中的立场性

Result: 识别出社区对医疗保健和医疗不信任的多个主题：认证与体现、对财务动机的怀疑、对健康AI意图的质疑。通过黑人女性主义思想重新构建研究发现，为具有历史性医疗不信任的社区提出健康自我管理技术的设计原则

Conclusion: 研究强调医疗不信任作为理性保护反应的重要性，提出基于黑人女性主义思想的设计原则，为具有历史性医疗不信任的社区开发健康自我管理技术提供指导，同时强调研究人员立场性反思在社区参与中的关键作用

Abstract: Despite increasing interest in culturally-sensitive health technologies, medical mistrust remains largely unexplored within human-centered computing. Considered a social determinant of health, medical mistrust is the belief that healthcare providers or institutions are acting against one's best interest. This is a rational, protective response based on historical context, structural inequities, and discrimination. To center race-based medical mistrust and the lived experiences of Black older adults with low income, we conducted interviews within publicly subsidized housing in the Southern United States. Our reflexive themes describe community perspectives on health care and medical mistrust, including accreditation and embodiment, skepticism of financial motivations, and the intentions behind health AI. We provide a reflective exercise for researchers to consider their positionality in relation to community engagements, and reframe our findings through Black Feminist Thought to propose design principles for health self-management technologies for communities with historically grounded medical mistrust.

</details>


### [174] [Human-centered Perspectives on a Clinical Decision Support System for Intensive Outpatient Veteran PTSD Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03467)
*Cynthia M. Baseman,Myeonghan Ryu,Nathaniel Swinger,Kefan Xu,Andrew M. Sherrill,Rosa I. Arriaga*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索为创伤后应激障碍（PTSD）延长暴露疗法（PE）设计临床决策支持系统（CDSS），通过访谈临床医生和退伍军人患者，识别系统机会与部署挑战，并从分布式认知等理论视角提出设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理治疗依赖于患者自我报告与临床直觉之间的协商，现有技术支持的证据表明有机会帮助调解这种张力。研究旨在探索为退伍军人PTSD的PE疗法设计CDSS的可能性，以辅助治疗决策过程。

Method: 采用两阶段访谈研究：1）收集执业PE临床医生的观点；2）收集曾接受PE治疗的美国退伍军人患者的观点。通过原型设计和质性分析，识别CDSS的机会与挑战，并运用分布式认知、情境学习和基础设施反转三个人类中心视角重构发现。

Result: 分析提炼出CDSS的机会（如提供作业概览、辅助患者概念化）和与上下文及部署相关的更大挑战（如导航退伍军人事务部系统）。通过理论视角重构，突出了在此背景下为心理治疗师设计CDSS的复杂性，并提出了与理论一致的设计考量。

Conclusion: 设计PE疗法的CDSS需要平衡技术辅助与临床直觉，考虑复杂的组织环境（如退伍军人事务部系统）。研究通过人类中心理论视角提供了设计考量，强调了在心理治疗中实施技术支持的复杂性，需要同时关注临床需求、组织基础设施和理论框架。

Abstract: Psychotherapy delivery relies on a negotiation between patient self-reports and clinical intuition. Growing evidence for technological support of psychotherapy suggests opportunities to aid the mediation of this tension. To explore this prospect, we designed a prototype of a clinical decision support system (CDSS) for treating veterans with post-traumatic stress disorder in a Prolonged Exposure (PE) therapy intensive outpatient program. We conducted a two-phase interview study to collect perspectives from practicing PE clinicians and former PE patients who are United States veterans. Our analysis distills opportunities for a CDSS (e.g., offering homework review at a glance, aiding patient conceptualization) and larger challenges related to context and deployment (e.g., navigating Veterans Affairs). By reframing our findings through three human-centered perspectives (distributed cognition, situated learning, infrastructural inversion), we highlight the complexities of designing a CDSS for psychotherapists in this context and offer theory-aligned design considerations.

</details>


### [175] [Inline Visualization and Manipulation of Real-Time Hardware Log for Supporting Debugging of Embedded Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03605)
*Andrea Bianchi,Zhi Lin Yap,Punn Lertjaturaphat,Austin Z. Henley,Kongpyung Justin Moon,Yoonji Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Inline是一款嵌入式系统调试工具，通过在代码中直接显示硬件日志来简化软件和硬件问题的调试过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arduino等嵌入式原型系统使交互设备开发更易上手，但调试困难，因为软件和硬件问题交织在一起。现有工具通常需要硬件仪器或通过串行监视器进行日志可视化，不够直观高效。

Method: 设计Inline编程工具，将硬件日志直接显示在代码中，提供实时执行流跟踪功能，并包含用于日志操作的表达式语言。

Result: 对12名用户进行的研究证明了该工具在辅助调试任务方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Inline通过将硬件日志直接集成到代码编辑环境中，简化了嵌入式系统的调试过程，提高了调试效率。

Abstract: The development of user-friendly embedded prototyping systems like Arduino has made creating interactive devices more accessible. However, debugging these systems is challenging due to the intertwined nature of software and hardware issues. Existing tools often require hardware instrumentation or log visualization through serial monitors. To address this, the authors designed Inline, a programming tool that simplifies debugging by displaying hardware logs directly within the code, providing real-time execution flow tracking and an expression language for log manipulation. A study with twelve users demonstrated the tool's effectiveness in aiding debugging tasks.

</details>


### [176] [Are You Comfortable Sharing It?: Leveraging Image Obfuscation Techniques to Enhance Sharing Privacy for Blind and Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03606)
*Satabdi Das,Nahian Beente Firuj,Manjot Singh,Arshad Nasser,Khalad Hasan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对盲人或视力障碍者(BVI)的图像分享隐私保护研究，评估不同过滤技术对敏感内容的处理效果，发现像素化最不受欢迎，过滤后的图像分享舒适度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人或视力障碍者(BVI)在分享图像时面临独特挑战，可能无意中分享包含敏感或不适当内容的图像，这会危及他们的隐私和人际关系。他们通常不了解分享此类内容的风险。

Method: 研究调查了图像过滤技术，帮助BVI用户在分享前管理敏感内容。对20名BVI参与者进行研究，评估应用于不同敏感度图像（如个人时刻或尴尬镜头）的各种过滤器。评估了不同分享对象（家人、朋友或陌生人）的情境。

Result: 像素化是最不受欢迎的方法，而其他过滤器的偏好因图像类型和分享情境而异。参与者报告称，与未过滤图像相比，分享过滤后的图像在所有受众中舒适度更高。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，提出了一套设计指南，以增强BVI个体的图像分享体验，帮助他们更好地管理敏感内容并保护隐私。

Abstract: People with Blind Visual Impairments (BVI) face unique challenges when sharing images, as these may accidentally contain sensitive or inappropriate content. In many instances, they are unaware of the potential risks associated with sharing such content, which can compromise their privacy and interpersonal relationships. To address this issue, we investigated image filtering techniques that could help BVI users manage sensitive content before sharing with various audiences, including family, friends, or strangers. We conducted a study with 20 BVI participants, evaluating different filters applied to images varying in sensitivity, such as personal moments or embarrassing shots. Results indicated that pixelation was the least preferred method, while preferences for other filters varied depending on image type and sharing context. Additionally, participants reported greater comfort when sharing filtered versus unfiltered images across audiences. Based on the results, we offer a set of design guidelines to enhance the image-sharing experience for BVI individuals.

</details>


### [177] [Modelling Visuo-Haptic Perception Change in Size Estimation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03614)
*Jian Zhang,Wafa Johal,Jarrod Knibbe*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究视觉-触觉尺寸感知随时间的变化，揭示感知漂移现象，探索视觉启动和航位推算的影响，并提出循环自调节感知模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多感官线索能准确感知物体属性，但解耦这些线索会产生感知差异，为交互创造新机会。然而，时间维度上的感知不仅依赖瞬时感官线索，还依赖先验信念，形成持续更新循环，这一循环机制及其对交互的影响尚不明确

Method: 通过80名参与者的实验，研究视觉-触觉尺寸感知随时间的变化，分析感知漂移现象，考察视觉启动和航位推算的影响

Result: 揭示了感知漂移现象，展示了视觉启动和航位推算对感知的影响，提出了视觉-触觉感知作为循环自调节系统的模型

Conclusion: 该研究不仅对VR中的错觉感知有直接影响，还揭示了视觉和触觉系统如何协同工作与分离，为理解多感官整合的时间动态提供了新视角

Abstract: Tangible interactions involve multiple sensory cues, enabling the accurate perception of object properties, such as size. Research has shown, however, that if we decouple these cues (for example, by altering the visual cue), then the resulting discrepancies present new opportunities for interactions. Perception over time though, not only relies on momentary sensory cues, but also on a priori beliefs about the object, implying a continuing update cycle. This cycle is poorly understood and its impact on interaction remains unknown. We study (N=80) visuo-haptic perception of size over time and (a) reveal how perception drifts, (b) examine the effects of visual priming and dead-reckoning, and (c) present a model of visuo-haptic perception as a cyclical, self-adjusting system. Our work has a direct impact on illusory perception in VR, but also sheds light on how our visual and haptic systems cooperate and diverge.

</details>


### [178] [Bridging Pedagogy and Play: Introducing a Language Mapping Interface for Human-AI Co-Creation in Educational Game Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03644)
*Daijin Yang,Erica Kleinman,Casper Harteveld*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个基于受控自然语言的网页工具，让用户与LLM协作创建结构化语言，将教学法映射到游戏玩法，旨在降低非专家设计师的教育游戏设计门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育游戏能培养批判性思维和问题解决能力，但教师难以设计出可靠实现特定学习目标的游戏。现有创作工具虽降低编程需求，但未解决教育游戏设计的根本挑战，且让非专家设计师过度依赖AI系统的不透明建议。

Method: 设计了一个基于受控自然语言框架的网页工具，将语言作为LLM辅助教育游戏设计的主要界面。用户与LLM助手协作开发结构化语言，通过四个关联组件将教学法映射到游戏玩法。

Result: 该工具通过使教学意图在界面中显式化且可编辑，有望降低非专家设计师的设计门槛，在关键决策中保持人类能动性，并在共同创作过程中及之后实现教学法与游戏玩法的对齐和反思。

Conclusion: 基于受控自然语言的协作框架为LLM辅助教育游戏设计提供了新途径，通过明确的教学意图表达和结构化语言设计，解决了现有工具在透明度、人类能动性和教学-游戏对齐方面的不足。

Abstract: Educational games can foster critical thinking, problem-solving, and motivation, yet instructors often find it difficult to design games that reliably achieve specific learning outcomes. Existing authoring environments reduce the need for programming expertise, but they do not eliminate the underlying challenges of educational game design, and they can leave non-expert designers reliant on opaque suggestions from AI systems. We designed a controlled natural language framework-based web tool that positions language as the primary interface for LLM-assisted educational game design. In the tool, users and an LLM assistant collaboratively develop a structured language that maps pedagogy to gameplay through four linked components. We argue that, by making pedagogical intent explicit and editable in the interface, the tool has the potential to lower design barriers for non-expert designers, preserves human agency in critical decisions, and enables alignment and reflections between pedagogy and gameplay during and after co-creation.

</details>


### [179] [Inclusive Mobile Learning: How Technology-Enabled Language Choice Supports Multilingual Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03675)
*Phenyo Phemelo Moletsane,Michael W. Asher,Christine Kwon,Paulo F. Carvalho,Amy Ogan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在乌干达进行的准实验研究表明，为学习者提供本地语言选项能显著提升教育技术的可及性，特别是对农村、低学历和低先验知识的学习者群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球大多数学习者是多语言的，但实施多语言教育在实践中仍然具有挑战性。教育技术（EdTech）为弥合这一差距、扩大语言多样化学习者的可及性提供了机会。

Method: 在乌干达对2,931名参与者进行准实验研究，这些参与者参加了一个基于广播和移动设备的非正式工程课程。学习者自选Leb Lango（当地语言）、英语或两者结合的混合选项作为教学语言。

Result: 1. Leb Lango版本课程被农村地区、正规教育程度较低、先验知识较少的学习者不成比例地使用，扩大了弱势学习者的参与度。
2. 提供Leb Lango教学与更高的主动参与度相关，即使是在注册英语教学的学习者中也是如此。
3. Leb Lango学习者初始表现较低，但学习增益更快，最终考试成绩与英语和混合学习者相当。

Conclusion: 为学习者提供本地语言选项是提高教育技术可及性的有效方式，能够扩大弱势群体的参与，促进更公平的学习成果。

Abstract: Most learners worldwide are multilingual, yet implementing multilingual education remains challenging in practice. EdTech offers an opportunity to bridge this gap and expand access for linguistically diverse learners. We conducted a quasi-experiment in Uganda with 2,931 participants enrolled in a non-formal radio- and mobile-based engineering course, where learners self-selected instruction in Leb Lango (a local language), English, or a Hybrid option combining both languages. The Leb Lango version of the course was used disproportionately by learners from rural areas, those with less formal education, and those with lower prior knowledge, broadening participation among disadvantaged learners. Moreover, the availability of Leb Lango instruction was associated with higher active participation, even among learners who registered for English instruction. Although Leb Lango learners began with lower performance, they demonstrated faster learning gains and achieved comparable final examination outcomes to English and Hybrid learners. These results suggest that providing local language options to learners is an effective way to make EdTech more accessible.

</details>


### [180] [Echoes of Norms: Investigating Counterspeech Bots' Influence on Bystanders in Online Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03687)
*Mengyao Wang,Shuai Ma,Nuo Li,Peng Zhang,Chenxin Li,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发了Civilbot反仇恨言论聊天机器人，通过混合方法研究其对旁观者的影响，发现策略选择对效果至关重要


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反仇恨言论提供了一种非压制性的在线社区管理方法，现有研究关注聊天机器人如何约束仇恨言论者和支持目标群体，但对旁观者的影响尚不清楚，需要填补这一研究空白

Method: 开发了反仇恨言论策略框架，构建了Civilbot聊天机器人，采用混合方法的被试内设计进行研究

Result: 旁观者普遍认为Civilbot可信且符合规范，但浅层推理限制了说服力；行为影响微妙：表现良好时可引导参与或替代行动，表现差时可能劝阻旁观者或激励其介入；认知策略（特别是配合积极语气）相对有效，策略与语境不匹配会削弱影响

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，为动员旁观者和塑造在线话语提供了设计见解，强调干预时机的重要性，以及通过推理驱动和语境感知策略进行有效干预的方法

Abstract: Counterspeech offers a non-repressive approach to moderate hate speech in online communities. Research has examined how counterspeech chatbots restrain hate speakers and support targets, but their impact on bystanders remains unclear. Therefore, we developed a counterspeech strategy framework and built \textit{Civilbot} for a mixed-method within-subjects study. Bystanders generally viewed Civilbot as credible and normative, though its shallow reasoning limited persuasiveness. Its behavioural effects were subtle: when performing well, it could guide participation or act as a stand-in; when performing poorly, it could discourage bystanders or motivate them to step in. Strategy proved critical: cognitive strategies that appeal to reason, especially when paired with a positive tone, were relatively effective, while mismatch of contexts and strategies could weaken impact. Based on these findings, we offer design insights for mobilizing bystanders and shaping online discourse, highlighting when to intervene and how to do so through reasoning-driven and context-aware strategies.

</details>


### [181] [Understanding Parents' Desires in Moderating Children's Interactions with GenAI Chatbots through LLM-Generated Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03727)
*John Driscoll,Yulin Chen,Viki Shi,Izak Vucharatavintara,Yaxing Yao,Haojian Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究父母如何希望调节儿童与生成式AI聊天机器人的互动，为未来GenAI家长控制工具设计提供依据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解父母对儿童与生成式AI聊天机器人互动的担忧和期望，为设计更有效的家长控制工具提供实证基础

Method: 1. 使用LLM生成合成儿童-GenAI互动场景，并由4位父母验证真实性；2. 从数据集中精选12个多样化、引发不同担忧程度且最真实的例子；3. 向24位父母展示这些例子，询问其担忧程度、原因、期望的修改方式和沟通方式

Result: 发现三个关键见解：1) 父母担忧当前GenAI聊天机器人家长控制忽略的互动类型；2) 父母希望获得对话级别的细粒度透明度和调节能力；3) 父母需要适应其策略和儿童年龄的个性化控制

Conclusion: 未来GenAI家长控制工具应关注当前被忽视的互动风险，提供细粒度的对话级透明度与调节，并支持个性化、适应性的控制策略

Abstract: This paper studies how parents want to moderate children's interactions with Generative AI chatbots, with the goal of informing the design of future GenAI parental control tools. We first used an LLM to generate synthetic child-GenAI chatbot interaction scenarios and worked with four parents to validate their realism. From this dataset, we carefully selected 12 diverse examples that evoked varying levels of concern and were rated the most realistic. Each example included a prompt and a GenAI chatbot response. We presented these to parents (N=24) and asked whether they found them concerning, why, and how they would prefer the responses to be modified and communicated. Our findings reveal three key insights: (1) parents express concern about interactions that current GenAI chatbot parental controls neglect; (2) parents want fine-grained transparency and moderation at the conversation level; and (3) parents need personalized controls that adapt to their desired strategies and children's ages.

</details>


### [182] [The Empty Quadrant: AI Teammates for Embodied Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04034)
*Hyein Kim,Sung Park*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出Field Atlas框架，挑战AIED领域40年来的"久坐假设"，将AI从信息传递工具转变为野外探究中的认知伙伴，通过轨迹建模而非成果评估学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIED研究四十年来一直基于"久坐假设"——学习者静止坐在屏幕前的设计承诺。现有移动学习和博物馆导览系统虽然让学习者进入物理空间，但主要将AI定位为信息传递工具而非认知伙伴。作者识别出这一理论空白：AI作为认知伙伴在非结构化、地点绑定的野外探究中的作用。

Method: 提出Field Atlas框架，基于4E认知理论（具身、嵌入、生成、扩展）、主动推理和双重编码理论。架构包括：自主摄影与即时语音反思结合；将AI限制为苏格拉底式启发而非答案提供；应用认知轨迹建模（ETM）将野外学习表示为物理-认知联合空间的连续轨迹。

Result: 通过博物馆场景演示该框架，展示生成的轨迹——绑定于特定身体、地点和时间——构成基于过程的证据，结构上抵抗AI伪造，提供了新的评估范式。

Conclusion: Field Atlas框架将AIED的指导隐喻从教学转向意义建构，重新定位AIED朝着具身、对话式的人-AI意义建构方向发展，为野外学习提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: For four decades, AIED research has rested on what we term the Sedentary Assumption: the unexamined design commitment to a stationary learner seated before a screen. Mobile learning and museum guides have moved learners into physical space, and context-aware systems have delivered location-triggered content -- yet these efforts predominantly cast AI in the role of information-de-livery tool rather than epistemic partner. We map this gap through a 2 x 2 matrix (AI Role x Learning Environment) and identify an undertheorized intersection: the configuration in which AI serves as an epistemic teammate during unstruc-tured, place-bound field inquiry and learning is assessed through trajectory rather than product. To fill it, we propose Field Atlas, a framework grounded in embod-ied, embedded, enactive, and extended (4E) cognition, active inference, and dual coding theory that shifts AIED's guiding metaphor from instruction to sensemak-ing. The architecture pairs volitional photography with immediate voice reflec-tion, constrains AI to Socratic provocation rather than answer delivery, and ap-plies Epistemic Trajectory Modeling (ETM) to represent field learning as a con-tinuous trajectory through conjoined physical-epistemic space. We demonstrate the framework through a museum scenario and argue that the resulting trajecto-ries -- bound to a specific body, place, and time -- constitute process-based evi-dence structurally resistant to AI fabrication, offering a new assessment paradigm and reorienting AIED toward embodied, dialogic human-AI sensemaking in the wild.

</details>


### [183] [Scrollytelling as an Alternative Format for Privacy Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04367)
*Gonzalo Gabriel Méndez,Jose Such*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了使用滚动叙事（scrollytelling）作为隐私政策呈现格式，通过将完整政策文本与动画视觉效果交织，创建动态阅读体验，相比纯文本改善了用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私政策通常冗长、复杂且很少被阅读，这限制了其在知情同意方面的有效性。需要探索更有效的隐私政策呈现格式来提高用户参与和理解。

Method: 开发了一个原型工具，将完整政策文本与动画视觉效果交织，创建滚动驱动的动态阅读体验。通过在线研究（N=454）比较了该工具与纯文本、两种营养标签变体以及独立交互可视化的效果。

Result: 滚动叙事相比纯文本改善了用户体验：提高了参与度、降低了认知负荷、增加了格式采用意愿和感知清晰度。在理解准确性和信心方面与其他格式相当，仅有一种营养标签变体表现略好。感知理解、透明度和信任的变化较小且统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 滚动叙事可以在保持理解能力的同时增强政策阅读体验。研究讨论了可访问政策沟通的设计意义，并确定了提高透明度和用户信任的方向。

Abstract: Privacy policies are long, complex, and rarely read, which limits their effectiveness in informed consent. We investigate scrollytelling, a scroll-driven narrative approach, as a privacy policy presentation format. We built a prototype that interleaves the full policy text with animated visuals to create a dynamic reading experience. In an online study (N=454), we compared our tool against text, two nutrition-label variants, and a standalone interactive visualization. Scrollytelling improved user experience over text, yielding higher engagement, lower cognitive load, greater willingness to adopt the format, and increased perceived clarity. It also matched other formats on comprehension accuracy and confidence, with only one nutrition-label variant performing slightly better. Changes in perceived understanding, transparency, and trust were small and statistically inconclusive. These findings suggest that scrollytelling can preserve comprehension while enhancing the experience of policy reading. We discuss design implications for accessible policy communication and identify directions for increasing transparency and user trust.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Not All Candidates are Created Equal: A Heterogeneity-Aware Approach to Pre-ranking in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03770)
*Pengfei Tong,Siyuan Chen,Chenwei Zhang,Bo Wang,Qi Pi,Pixun Li,Zuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HAP框架通过冲突敏感采样和自适应计算分配解决预排序阶段异构样本的梯度冲突问题，在工业推荐系统中实现性能提升且不增加计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预排序阶段面临来自粗粒度检索结果、细粒度排序信号和曝光反馈的异构训练样本混合问题，现有方法不加区分地混合这些样本导致梯度冲突：困难样本主导训练而简单样本利用不足，造成性能次优；同时均匀扩展模型复杂度对所有样本效率低下，在简单样本上过度计算且训练缓慢。

Method: 提出异构感知自适应预排序（HAP）框架：1）通过冲突敏感采样和定制损失设计缓解梯度冲突；2）自适应分配候选者间的计算预算；3）分离简单和困难样本，为每个子集设计专用优化路径；4）对所有候选者应用轻量级模型实现高效覆盖，对困难样本进一步使用更强模型，在保持准确性的同时降低成本。

Result: HAP已在今日头条生产系统部署9个月，用户应用使用时长提升最高0.4%，活跃天数提升0.05%，且未增加额外计算成本；同时发布了大规模工业混合样本数据集，支持预排序中源驱动候选异构性的系统研究。

Conclusion: HAP框架有效解决了预排序阶段的异构样本梯度冲突问题，通过分离样本类型和自适应计算分配，在提升预排序效果的同时为工业推荐系统的扩展策略提供了实用视角。

Abstract: Most large-scale recommender systems follow a multi-stage cascade of retrieval, pre-ranking, ranking, and re-ranking. A key challenge at the pre-ranking stage arises from the heterogeneity of training instances sampled from coarse-grained retrieval results, fine-grained ranking signals, and exposure feedback. Our analysis reveals that prevailing pre-ranking methods, which indiscriminately mix heterogeneous samples, suffer from gradient conflicts: hard samples dominate training while easy ones remain underutilized, leading to suboptimal performance. We further show that the common practice of uniformly scaling model complexity across all samples is inefficient, as it overspends computation on easy cases and slows training without proportional gains. To address these limitations, this paper presents Heterogeneity-Aware Adaptive Pre-ranking (HAP), a unified framework that mitigates gradient conflicts through conflict-sensitive sampling coupled with tailored loss design, while adaptively allocating computational budgets across candidates. Specifically, HAP disentangles easy and hard samples, directing each subset along dedicated optimization paths. Building on this separation, it first applies lightweight models to all candidates for efficient coverage, and further engages stronger models on the hard ones, maintaining accuracy while reducing cost. This approach not only improves pre-ranking effectiveness but also provides a practical perspective on scaling strategies in industrial recommender systems. HAP has been deployed in the Toutiao production system for 9 months, yielding up to 0.4% improvement in user app usage duration and 0.05% in active days, without additional computational cost. We also release a large-scale industrial hybrid-sample dataset to enable the systematic study of source-driven candidate heterogeneity in pre-ranking.

</details>


### [185] [CAMMSR: Category-Guided Attentive Mixture of Experts for Multimodal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04320)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Yijie Li,Jianheng Tang,Yunhuai Liu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CAMMSR：一种基于类别引导的注意力专家混合模型，用于多模态序列推荐，通过动态模态权重分配和跨模态协同建模提升推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐模型大多依赖启发式融合策略，无法捕捉用户-模态交互的动态性和上下文敏感性。用户对模态的偏好不仅因人而异，同一用户在不同物品或类别间也会变化。此外，模态间的协同效应（组合信号触发孤立模态无法产生的用户兴趣）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出CAMMSR模型，核心是类别引导的注意力专家混合模块，从多视角学习专门化的物品表示并显式建模模态间协同效应。该组件通过辅助类别预测任务引导动态分配模态权重，实现多模态信号的自适应融合。此外，设计了模态交换对比学习任务，通过序列级增强提升跨模态表示对齐。

Result: 在四个公共数据集上的广泛实验表明，CAMMSR始终优于最先进的基线方法，验证了其在实现自适应、协同和以用户为中心的多模态序列推荐方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CAMMSR通过类别引导的动态模态权重分配和显式模态协同建模，解决了现有多模态序列推荐模型的局限性，实现了更精准的个性化推荐。

Abstract: The explosion of multimedia data in information-rich environments has intensified the challenges of personalized content discovery, positioning recommendation systems as an essential form of passive data management. Multimodal sequential recommendation, which leverages diverse item information such as text and images, has shown great promise in enriching item representations and deepening the understanding of user interests. However, most existing models rely on heuristic fusion strategies that fail to capture the dynamic and context-sensitive nature of user-modal interactions. In real-world scenarios, user preferences for modalities vary not only across individuals but also within the same user across different items or categories. Moreover, the synergistic effects between modalities-where combined signals trigger user interest in ways isolated modalities cannot-remain largely underexplored.
  To this end, we propose CAMMSR, a Category-guided Attentive Mixture of Experts model for Multimodal Sequential Recommendation. At its core, CAMMSR introduces a category-guided attentive mixture of experts (CAMoE) module, which learns specialized item representations from multiple perspectives and explicitly models inter-modal synergies. This component dynamically allocates modality weights guided by an auxiliary category prediction task, enabling adaptive fusion of multimodal signals. Additionally, we design a modality swap contrastive learning task to enhance cross-modal representation alignment through sequence-level augmentation. Extensive experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that CAMMSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating its effectiveness in achieving adaptive, synergistic, and user-centric multimodal sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [186] [DisenReason: Behavior Disentanglement and Latent Reasoning for Shared-Account Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03782)
*Jiawei Cheng,Min Gao,Zongwei Wang,Xiaofei Zhu,Zhiyi Liu,Wentao Li,Wei Li,Huan Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DisenReason：针对共享账户序列推荐的两阶段推理方法，通过行为解耦和潜在用户推理解决共享账户中用户数量不确定的问题，显著提升推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享账户序列推荐方法通常假设每个账户有固定数量的潜在用户，这限制了模型适应多样化共享模式的能力，降低了推荐准确性。需要一种能够动态推断账户背后用户数量的方法。

Method: 提出DisenReason两阶段推理方法：1）从频域视角进行行为解耦，创建统一账户行为表示；2）基于该表示进行潜在用户推理，推断账户背后的用户数量。该方法将推断用户数量问题转化为生成一系列中间嵌入的过程。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，DisenReason在所有指标上均优于现有最先进基线方法，在MRR@5上相对提升高达12.56%，在Recall@20上相对提升高达6.06%。

Conclusion: DisenReason通过两阶段推理有效解决了共享账户序列推荐中用户数量不确定的问题，显著提升了推荐性能，为共享账户推荐提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Shared-account usage is common on streaming and e-commerce platforms, where multiple users share one account. Existing shared-account sequential recommendation (SSR) methods often assume a fixed number of latent users per account, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse sharing patterns and reducing recommendation accuracy. Recent latent reasoning technique applied in sequential recommendation (SR) generate intermediate embeddings from the user embedding (e.g, last item embedding) to uncover users' potential interests, which inspires us to treat the problem of inferring the number of latent users as generating a series of intermediate embeddings, shifting from inferring preferences behind user to inferring the users behind account. However, the last item cannot be directly used for reasoning in SSR, as it can only represent the behavior of the most recent latent user, rather than the collective behavior of the entire account. To address this, we propose DisenReason, a two-stage reasoning method tailored to SSR. DisenReason combines behavior disentanglement stage from frequency-domain perspective to create a collective and unified account behavior representation, which serves as a pivot for latent user reasoning stage to infer the number of users behind the account. Experiments on four benchmark datasets show that DisenReason consistently outperforms all state-of-the-art baselines across four benchmark datasets, achieving relative improvements of up to 12.56\% in MRR@5 and 6.06\% in Recall@20.

</details>


### [187] [SORT: A Systematically Optimized Ranking Transformer for Industrial-scale Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03988)
*Chunqi Wang,Bingchao Wu,Taotian Pang,Jiahao Wang,Jie Yang,Jia Liu,Hao Zhang,Hai Zhu,Lei Shen,Shizhun Wang,Bing Wang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SORT是一个专为工业级排序模型设计的可扩展Transformer，通过优化解决了高特征稀疏性和低标签密度问题，在电商场景中实现了显著的性能提升和延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在LLMs中取得了显著成功，但在工业级排序模型中的应用仍处于早期阶段，主要受到高特征稀疏性和低标签密度两大挑战的阻碍。

Method: 提出SORT模型，采用请求中心样本组织、局部注意力、查询剪枝和生成式预训练等技术解决特征稀疏和标签密度问题；对tokenization、MHA和FFN模块进行一系列改进以稳定训练并扩大模型容量；优化训练系统将MFU提升至22%。

Result: SORT在实验中优于强基线，在数据规模、模型规模和序列长度方面展现出优秀的可扩展性；在线A/B测试显示，在大型电商场景中，订单量提升6.35%，买家数提升5.97%，GMV提升5.47%，同时延迟降低44.67%，吞吐量提升121.33%。

Conclusion: SORT成功地将Transformer的优势引入工业级排序模型，通过系统性优化解决了实际部署中的关键挑战，实现了显著的商业指标提升和系统性能优化。

Abstract: While Transformers have achieved remarkable success in LLMs through superior scalability, their application in industrial-scale ranking models remains nascent, hindered by the challenges of high feature sparsity and low label density. In this paper, we propose SORT (Systematically Optimized Ranking Transformer), a scalable model designed to bridge the gap between Transformers and industrial-scale ranking models. We address the high feature sparsity and low label density challenges through a series of optimizations, including request-centric sample organization, local attention, query pruning and generative pre-training. Furthermore, we introduce a suite of refinements to the tokenization, multi-head attention (MHA), and feed-forward network (FFN) modules, which collectively stabilize the training process and enlarge the model capacity. To maximize hardware efficiency, we optimize our training system to elevate the model FLOPs utilization (MFU) to 22%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SORT outperforms strong baselines and exhibits excellent scalability across data size, model size and sequence length, while remaining flexible at integrating diverse features. Finally, online A/B testing in large-scale e-commerce scenarios confirms that SORT achieves significant gains in key business metrics, including orders (+6.35%), buyers (+5.97%) and GMV (+5.47%), while simultaneously halving latency (-44.67%) and doubling throughput (+121.33%).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Knowledge Graph and Hypergraph Transformers with Repository-Attention and Journey-Based Role Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03304)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种联合训练句子和结构化数据的简洁架构，保持知识与语言表示分离，通过键值存储库和基于旅程的角色传输实现结构化数据与语言模型的统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将知识图谱和语言表示紧密耦合，缺乏可解释性和模块化。需要一种能够同时处理句子和结构化数据，同时保持知识与语言表示明确分离的架构。

Method: 将知识图谱和超图视为具有角色槽的结构化实例，编码到键值存储库中供语言Transformer关注。采用基于旅程的角色传输统一边标记KG遍历、超边遍历和句子结构。使用双流架构、分层层组（实例局部、邻域和全局混合注意力）、独立存储库检索，以及跨掩码语言建模、链接预测和角色一致性去噪的多任务目标。

Result: 实现了语言上下文和结构化知识之间的显式、可检查分离，同时通过交叉注意力实现紧密对齐。模型能够统一处理多种结构化数据形式，保持模块化和可解释性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种创新的联合训练架构，成功实现了语言表示和结构化知识的显式分离与有效对齐，为可解释的知识增强语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a concise architecture for joint training on sentences and structured data while keeping knowledge and language representations separable. The model treats knowledge graphs and hypergraphs as structured instances with role slots and encodes them into a key-value repository that a language transformer can attend over. Attention is conditioned by journey-based role transport, which unifies edge-labeled KG traversal, hyperedge traversal, and sentence structure. We outline a dual-stream architecture, hierarchical layer groups with instance-local, neighborhood, and global mixing attention, retrieval over a separate repository, and multi-task objectives spanning masked language modeling, link prediction, and role-consistency denoising. The result is an explicit, inspectable separation between linguistic context and structured knowledge, while still enabling tight alignment through cross-attention.

</details>


### [189] [RADAR: Learning to Route with Asymmetry-aware DistAnce Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03388)
*Hang Yi,Ziwei Huang,Yining Ma,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RADAR是一个可扩展的神经框架，通过SVD分解和Sinkhorn归一化增强现有神经VRP求解器处理非对称距离矩阵的能力，在非对称车辆路径问题上表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经求解器主要假设对称欧几里得距离，限制了在现实场景中的应用。核心挑战是如何编码非对称距离矩阵中的关系特征，早期方法直接编码这些矩阵但往往无法产生紧凑嵌入且泛化能力差。

Method: RADAR从静态和动态两个角度处理非对称性：1) 使用奇异值分解(SVD)对非对称距离矩阵进行初始化，生成紧凑且可泛化的嵌入，固有编码每个节点的入站和出站成本；2) 在编码过程中，用Sinkhorn归一化替代标准softmax，在注意力权重中施加联合行和列距离感知，建模嵌入交互的动态非对称性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试中，RADAR在各种VRP上均优于强基线方法，在分布内和分布外实例上都表现出色，展示了在解决非对称VRP问题上的鲁棒泛化能力和优越性能。

Conclusion: RADAR成功解决了神经VRP求解器处理非对称输入的关键挑战，通过创新的静态和动态非对称性建模方法，为现实世界非对称车辆路径问题提供了有效的神经解决方案。

Abstract: Recent neural solvers have achieved strong performance on vehicle routing problems (VRPs), yet they mainly assume symmetric Euclidean distances, restricting applicability to real-world scenarios. A core challenge is encoding the relational features in asymmetric distance matrices of VRPs. Early attempts directly encoded these matrices but often failed to produce compact embeddings and generalized poorly at scale. In this paper, we propose RADAR, a scalable neural framework that augments existing neural VRP solvers with the ability to handle asymmetric inputs. RADAR addresses asymmetry from both static and dynamic perspectives. It leverages Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) on the asymmetric distance matrix to initialize compact and generalizable embeddings that inherently encode the static asymmetry in the inbound and outbound costs of each node. To further model dynamic asymmetry in embedding interactions during encoding, it replaces the standard softmax with Sinkhorn normalization that imposes joint row and column distance awareness in attention weights. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across various VRPs show that RADAR outperforms strong baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution instances, demonstrating robust generalization and superior performance in solving asymmetric VRPs.

</details>


### [190] [Heterogeneous Time Constants Improve Stability in Equilibrium Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03402)
*Yoshimasa Kubo,Suhani Pragnesh Modi,Smit Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 引入异质时间步长（HTS）改进平衡传播（EP），通过为神经元分配特定时间常数提升训练稳定性，同时保持任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平衡传播模型使用均匀标量时间步长，这与生物神经元膜时间常数的异质性不符。为增强平衡传播的生物合理性和鲁棒性，需要引入更符合生物现实的异质时间步长。

Method: 为平衡传播引入异质时间步长（HTS），为每个神经元分配从生物启发的分布中抽取的特定时间常数，替代原有的均匀标量时间步长。

Result: 异质时间步长提高了训练稳定性，同时保持了与原有方法竞争的任务性能。这表明异质时间步长能同时增强平衡传播的生物合理性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 引入异质时间步长是平衡传播的重要改进，它使模型更符合生物神经元的异质时间特性，同时提升了训练稳定性而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) is a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation for training neural networks. However, existing EP models use a uniform scalar time step dt, which corresponds biologically to a membrane time constant that is heterogeneous across neurons. Here, we introduce heterogeneous time steps (HTS) for EP by assigning neuron-specific time constants drawn from biologically motivated distributions. We show that HTS improves training stability while maintaining competitive task performance. These results suggest that incorporating heterogeneous temporal dynamics enhances both the biological realism and robustness of equilibrium propagation.

</details>


### [191] [A Short Note on a Variant of the Squint Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03409)
*Haipeng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Squint算法的简单变体，通过修改证明获得与Freund等人NormalHedge变体相似的遗憾界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Koolen和Van Erven的Squint算法，使其获得与近期Freund等人NormalHedge变体相似的遗憾界

Method: 设计Squint算法的简单变体，并对原始证明进行同等简单的修改

Result: 证明了该变体能够确保与Freund等人NormalHedge变体相似的遗憾界

Conclusion: 通过简单修改Squint算法及其证明，获得了与近期研究相似的遗憾界结果

Abstract: This short note describes a simple variant of the Squint algorithm of Koolen and Van Erven [2015] for the classic expert problem. Via an equally simple modification of their proof, we prove that this variant ensures a regret bound that resembles the one shown in a recent work by Freund et al. [2026] for a variant of the NormalHedge algorithm [Chaudhuri et al., 2009].

</details>


### [192] [[Re] FairDICE: A Gap Between Theory And Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03454)
*Peter Adema,Karim Galliamov,Aleksey Evstratovskiy,Ross Geurts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文是对FairDICE算法的复制研究，发现其理论主张基本成立，但代码存在错误导致在连续环境中退化为标准行为克隆，且超参数未充分指定。修复后实验显示FairDICE可扩展到复杂环境和高维奖励，但依赖在线超参数调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中多目标平衡缺乏有效的公平妥协方法，FairDICE声称能填补这一空白。本研究旨在验证FairDICE的可复制性，评估其理论主张和实际性能。

Method: 采用复制研究方法，分析FairDICE的理论主张，检查原始代码实现，识别并修复代码错误（特别是连续环境中的问题），重新指定未充分定义的超参数，并进行扩展实验验证。

Result: 理论主张基本成立，但发现代码错误使FairDICE在连续环境中退化为标准行为克隆。修复后实验表明：1）FairDICE可扩展到复杂环境和高维奖励；2）算法对在线超参数调优有较强依赖；3）原始论文的实验论证需要重大修订。

Conclusion: FairDICE在理论上是值得关注的方法，其实验论证需要显著改进。复制研究揭示了实现细节的重要性，并展示了修复后算法在复杂环境中的潜力，但强调了超参数调优的依赖性。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) is an emerging field of RL in which policies are learned solely from demonstrations. Within offline RL, some environments involve balancing multiple objectives, but existing multi-objective offline RL algorithms do not provide an efficient way to find a fair compromise. FairDICE (see arXiv:2506.08062v2) seeks to fill this gap by adapting OptiDICE (an offline RL algorithm) to automatically learn weights for multiple objectives to e.g.\ incentivise fairness among objectives. As this would be a valuable contribution, this replication study examines the replicability of claims made regarding FairDICE. We find that many theoretical claims hold, but an error in the code reduces FairDICE to standard behaviour cloning in continuous environments, and many important hyperparameters were originally underspecified. After rectifying this, we show in experiments extending the original paper that FairDICE can scale to complex environments and high-dimensional rewards, though it can be reliant on (online) hyperparameter tuning. We conclude that FairDICE is a theoretically interesting method, but the experimental justification requires significant revision.

</details>


### [193] [Half the Nonlinearity Is Wasted: Measuring and Reallocating the Transformer's MLP Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03459)
*Peter Balogh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer MLP非线性并非总是必要，通过门控机制可识别并替换近线性MLP计算，在GPT-2中实现25-56%线性路由且困惑度损失小于1%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer MLP非线性何时真正必要，旨在识别并减少不必要的非线性计算，提高模型效率

Method: 使用带d+1参数的门控机制决定何时用线性替代器替换完整MLP；在6个模型(162M-2.8B参数)、两种架构、三个语料库上进行系统实验；通过逐步替换中间层MLP为冻结线性矩阵进行概念验证

Result: 非线性需求无法从token身份预测(跨语料库相关性r<0.05)；路由决策完全依赖上下文；GPT-2中25-56%线性路由仅带来<1%困惑度成本；GPT-2 Large中11/36层通过门控超越基线；Pythia模型成本较高但2.8B版本有一个层略胜基线；5/24层可零成本线性化；完全训练预算下4个线性化层带来10.2%困惑度提升，两阶段门控方法提升至17.3%

Conclusion: Transformer MLP非线性并非总是必要，门控机制能有效识别并替换近线性计算；某些层的非线性MLP甚至有害，线性化可带来性能提升；该效果具有架构依赖性

Abstract: We investigate when transformer MLP nonlinearity is actually necessary. A gate with $d+1$ parameters decides when to replace the full MLP with a linear surrogate. Through systematic investigation across six models (162M-2.8B parameters), two architectures, and three corpora, we establish that nonlinearity need cannot be predicted from token identity: cross-corpus correlation is zero ($r < 0.05$). The routing decision is fully contextual. Despite weak per-instance predictability, the gate exploits a heavily skewed distribution where most MLP computations are near-linear, achieving 25-56% linear routing at <1% perplexity cost in GPT-2. In GPT-2 Large, 11 of 36 layers beat baseline with gating and no layer exceeds 3.7% all-linear cost. This success is architecture-dependent: Pythia models show higher costs, though Pythia-2.8B's full 32-layer sweep reveals one layer that narrowly beats baseline. As a proof of concept, we progressively replace middle-layer MLPs with frozen linear matrices: 5 of 24 layers linearize at zero cost. With a full training budget, 4 linearized layers yield a 10.2% perplexity improvement -- and a two-phase gated approach pushes this to 17.3%, beating a vanilla fine-tuning control and confirming that the nonlinear MLPs at these layers were actively harmful.

</details>


### [194] [Minimax Optimal Strategy for Delayed Observations in Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03480)
*Harin Lee,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合增强方法和置信上界的方法来处理状态观测延迟的强化学习问题，获得了最优的遗憾界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
